box, bb, tl end local function RemoveEntry(inst) local e = Tracked[inst] if not e then return end Tracked[inst] = nil if e.Conn then pcall(e.Conn.Disconnect, e.Conn); e.Conn = nil end Mode.Free(e) end local function HideEntry(e) e.Shown = false if not e.HL and not e.Line then return end if e.HL then e.HL.Enabled = false e.Box.Visible = false e.BB.Enabled = false end if e.Line then e.Line.Visible = false end local now = os.clock() if not e.HidSince then e.HidSince = now elseif now - e.HidSince > 5 then Mode.Free(e) end end local OnEntitySeen local ExtraSerialize, ExtraApply local PRIORITY = { 6, players = 5, stairs = 1, exit = 4, cart = 4, } local Watch = { Limited = { Stem = true, Drone = true, Drones = true }, M = {}, Loop = false, Gold = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" }) } local "\100\105\114\116", "\104\105\108\108", "\115\112\097\119\110", "\109\097\100\114\105\103\117\101\114\097" } function Watch.IsBurrowName(name) local n = name:lower() for _, w in ipairs(BURROW_WORDS) do if n:find(w, 1, true) then return true end end return false end function Watch.IsBurrow(inst) local p, i = inst, 0 while p and p ~= Workspace and i < 5 do local n = p.Name:lower() if i + 1 end return false end function Watch.Run() if Watch.Loop then return end Watch.Loop = true task.spawn(function() while next(Watch.M) do task.wait(0.2) local char = LocalPlayer.Character local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("\072\117\109\097\110\111\105\100\082\111\111\116\080\097\114\116") for inst, w in pairs(Watch.M) do if not inst.Parent then Watch.M[inst] = nil elseif hrp then local ok, pos = pcall(function() return inst:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") and inst:GetPivot().Position or inst.Position end) if ok and pos then if not w.Start then w.Start = pos elseif (pos - w.Start).Magnitude > 3 and (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude <= 50 then Watch.M[inst] = nil Watch.Promote(inst, w.Opts) end end end end end Watch.Loop = false end) end local function Register(inst, cat, label, opts) if not Active[cat] then return end if cat == "\101\110\116\105\116\105\101\115" and label == "\077\097\110\100\114\097\107\101" and not (opts and opts.Direct) then if not Tracked[inst] and not Watch.M[inst] and not Watch.IsBurrowName(inst.Name) then Watch.M[inst] = { Opts = opts } Watch.Run() end return end local existing = Tracked[inst] if existing then if (PRIORITY[cat] or 3) > (PRIORITY[existing.Cat] or 3) then RemoveEntry(inst) else return end end local part = (opts and opts.Part) or GetPart(inst) if not part then return end local conn = inst.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent) if not parent then RemoveEntry(inst) end end) Tracked[inst] = { Inst = inst, Cat = cat, Label = label, Part = part, Conn = conn, Known = opts and opts.Known, Key = opts and opts.Key, Prompt = opts and opts.Prompt, GeoT = 0, NoGeo = false, RoomNum = opts and opts.RoomNum, Door = opts and opts.Door, Dist = 0, Shown = false, } if OnEntitySeen and (cat == "\101\110\116\105\116\105\101\115" or cat == "\100\117\112\101") and opts and opts.Known then pcall(OnEntitySeen, opts.Key or label, inst) end if cat == "\105\116\101\109\115" and Watch.OnItem then pcall(Watch.OnItem, label, inst, opts and opts.Known, opts and opts.Key) end if Cfg.DebugVerbose then Dbg("\082\069\071\058" .. cat, inst, "\108\097\098\101\108\061" .. tostring(label)) end end function Watch.Promote(inst, opts) local o = {} for k, v in pairs(opts or {}) do o[k] = v end o.Direct = true Register(inst, "\101\110\116\105\116\105\101\115", "\077\097\110\100\114\097\107\101", o) end function Watch.RegisterGold(target) if Tracked[target] or Watch.Gold[target] or Watch.IsBurrow(target) then return end Watch.Gold[target] = true task.spawn(function() for _ = 1, 15 do if not target.Parent then break end local prompt = target:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116", true) if prompt and GetPart(target) then local visible = false then visible = true break end end if visible and prompt.Enabled and prompt.MaxActivationDistance > 0 then local value = target:GetAttribute("\071\111\108\100\086\097\108\117\101") Register(target, "\103\111\108\100", value and ("\071\111\108\100\032\091" .. value .. "\093") or "\071\111\108\100", { Prompt = prompt }) end break end task.wait(0.3) end Watch.Gold[target] = nil end) end local CurrentRooms local function HasTrackedAncestor(inst) local p = inst.Parent while p and p ~= CurrentRooms and p ~= Workspace do if Tracked[p] then return true end p = p.Parent end return false end local function RegisterWhenReady(inst, cat, label) Register(inst, cat, label) if Tracked[inst] or GetPart(inst) then return end task.spawn(function() for _ = 1, 30 do task.wait(0.2) if not then Register(target, "\115\116\097\114\100\117\115\116", STARDUST_NAMES[n]) elseif GOLD_NAMES[n] then Watch.RegisterGold(target) else return false end return true end local STAIR_ASSETS = { false end local function StairOwner(inst) local p, i, found, cat, label = inst, 0, nil, nil, nil while p and p ~= Workspace and i < 10 do local def = STAIR_ASSETS[p.Name] if def and end p, i = p.Parent, i + 1 end return found, cat, label end local function RegisterCrusher(inst) local function try() if Tracked[inst] then return true end if GetPart(inst) then Register(inst, 1, 30 do task.wait(0.2) if not inst.Parent then return end if try() then return end end end) end local GENERIC_PARTS = { Handle = true, Hitbox = true, Base = true, Main = true, Part = true, Model = HintPrompt = true, InteractPrompt = true, } local HIDE_EXACT = { Wardrobe = "\067\108\111\115\101\116", Toolshed = "\084\111\111\108\032\083\104\101\100" } local function HideLabel(name) if (name:gsub("\095", "\032")) end local CHEST_EXACT = { ChestBox = "\067\104\101\115\116", ChestBoxLocked = "\076\111\099\107\101\100\032\067\104\101\115\116" } local CONTAINER_WORDS = { "\077\097\107\101\115\104\105\102\116\032\068\114\097\119\101\114" or "\068\114\097\119\101\114" end end end local function IsKnownObject(name) if KEY_NAMES[name] or OBJECTIVE_NAMES[name] or IsContainerModel(m) if not m then return false end if ClassifyContainer(m.Name) then return true end local e = Tracked[m] return e ~= nil and (e.Cat == "\100\114\097\119\101\114\115" or e.Cat == "\108\111\099\107\101\114\115" or e.Cat == "\099\104\101\115\116\115" or e.Cat == "\119\097\114\100\114\111\098\101\115") end local function ResolveTarget(prompt) local t = prompt.Parent if t and t:IsA("\065\116\116\097\099\104\109\101\110\116") then t = t.Parent end if not t then return nil end if IsKnownObject(t.Name) then return t end if t:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then local pm = t.Parent if pm and pm:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") and pm.Parent ~= CurrentRooms and not IsContainerModel(pm) then t = pm end end while GENERIC_PARTS[t.Name] and t.Parent and t.Parent:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") and t.Parent.Parent ~= CurrentRooms and not IsContainerModel(t.Parent) and not IsKnownObject(t.Name) do t = t.Parent end return t end local function CleanName(name) return (name:gsub("\095", "\032"):gsub("\040\037\108\041\040\037\117\041", "\037\049\032\037\050")) end local function ProcessContainerPrompt(prompt) local so, sc, sl = return end if CurrentRooms and t.Parent == CurrentRooms and not OBJECTIVE_NAMES[t.Name] then return end if RegisterByName(t) then return end if HasTrackedAncestor(t) then return end local low = Norm(t.Name) if low == "\100\111\111\114" or low == "\108\111\099\107" or low:find("\100\117\112\101", 1, true) then return end if t:FindFirstChild("\072\105\100\101\080\114\111\109\112\116", true) then return end local cat, label = ClassifyContainer(t.Name) if cat then Register(t, cat, label, { Prompt = prompt }) elseif prompt.Name == "\076\111\111\116\080\114\111\109\112\116" then Register(t, "\100\114\097\119\101\114\115", CleanName(t.Name), { Prompt = prompt }) Dbg("\067\079\078\084\065\073\078\069\082\063", t, "\076\111\111\116\080\114\111\109\112\116\032\109\111\100\101\061" .. Mode.Name) else Register(t, "\105\110\116\101\114\097\099\116\097\098\108\101\115", CleanName(t.Name), { Prompt = prompt }) Dbg("\065\067\084\073\086\065\084\069\063", t, "\109\111\100\101\061" .. Mode.Name) end end local function Process(inst) if inst:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then local pn = inst.Name if pn == "\072\105\100\101\080\114\111\109\112\116" then if not Active.wardrobes then return end local m = inst:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("\077\111\100\101\108") if m and m ~= CurrentRooms and m.Parent ~= CurrentRooms then if not HIDE_NAMES[m.Name] then Dbg("\072\073\068\069\063", m) end Register(m, "\119\097\114\100\114\111\098\101\115", HideLabel(m.Name)) end elseif pn == "\077\111\100\117\108\101\080\114\111\109\112\116" or pn == "\072\101\114\098\080\114\111\109\112\116" then if not (Active.items or Active.keys or Active.objectives or Active.gold or Active.stardust or Active.glitch or Active.lotus or Active.scanner) then return end local t = ResolveTarget(inst) if not t then return end if Watch.IsBurrow(t) or Watch.IsBurrow(inst) then return end if RegisterByName(t) then return end if Tracked[t] and not IsContainerModel(t) then return end local nn = Norm(t.Name) if nn:find("\115\097\108\108\121", 1, true) then RegisterWhenReady(t, "\111\098\106\101\099\116\105\118\101\115", "\083\097\108\108\121\039\115\032\084\111\121") return end local objText = inst.ObjectText local cands = { t.Name, t:GetAttribute("\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101"), objText } if pn == "\072\101\114\098\080\114\111\109\112\116" then cands[#cands + 1] = "\071\114\101\101\110\032\072\101\114\098" end local display = ResolveItem(cands) if not display then local blob = Norm(t.Name) .. "\124" .. Norm(tostring(t:GetAttribute("\068\105\115\112\108\097\121\078\097\109\101") or "")) .. "\124" .. Norm(tostring(objText or "")) if blob:find("\103\108\105\116\099\104", 1, true) then display = "\071\108\105\116\099\104\032\070\114\097\103\109\101\110\116" elseif blob:find("\115\116\097\114\100\117\115\116", 1, true) then display = "\083\116\097\114\100\117\115\116" elseif blob:find("\109\111\100\117\108\101", 1, true) and blob:find("\115\099\097\110", 1, true) then display = "\083\099\097\110\110\101\114\032\077\111\100\117\108\101" end end if not display and Mode.Name == "\079\117\116\100\111\111\114\115" then return end if Cfg.Debug then local byName, byText = ResolveItem({ t.Name }), ResolveItem({ objText }) if byName and byText and byName ~= byText then Dbg("\073\084\069\077\045\077\073\083\077\065\084\067\072", t, "\109\111\100\101\108\061" .. byName .. "\032\116\101\120\116\061" .. byText) end end if not display then Dbg("\073\084\069\077\063", t, "\112\114\111\109\112\116\061" .. pn .. "\032\111\098\106\101\099\116\084\101\120\116\061" .. tostring(objText)) end local special = display and SPECIAL_CATS[display] if special then Register(t, special, display, { Known = true, Key = display }) else Register(t, "\105\116\101\109\115", display or CleanName(t.Name), { Known = display ~= nil, Key = display }) end elseif pn == "\065\099\116\105\118\097\116\101\069\118\101\110\116\080\114\111\109\112\116" or pn == "\076\111\111\116\080\114\111\109\112\116" then if not (Active.drawers or Active.lockers or Active.interactables) then return end ProcessContainerPrompt(inst) elseif not KNOWN_PROMPTS[pn] then Dbg("\080\082\079\077\080\084\063", inst.Parent or inst, "\112\114\111\109\112\116\061" .. pn .. "\032\109\111\100\101\061" .. Mode.Name) end return end if inst:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or inst:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then if inst.Name == "\067\114\117\115\104\101\114" then local pr = inst.Parent if Active.entities and pr and pr.Name == "\083\116\097\105\114\119\101\108\108\067\114\117\115\104\101\114" and CurrentRooms and inst:IsDescendantOf(CurrentRooms) then RegisterCrusher(inst) return end else local sd = STAIR_ASSETS[inst.Name] if sd and Active[sd[1]] then local owner = StairOwner(inst) if owner then RegisterWhenReady(owner, sd[1], sd[2]) end return end end if RegisterByName(inst) then return end if inst:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then local n = inst.Name local lab = (Active.entities and Cfg.RoomEntities and inst.Parent ~= Workspace) and EntityLabel(n, true) or nil if HIDE_EXACT[n] then Register(inst, "\119\097\114\100\114\111\098\101\115", HIDE_EXACT[n]) elseif lab and not HasTrackedAncestor(inst) then Register(inst, "\101\110\116\105\116\105\101\115", lab, { Known = true, Key = lab }) else local cl = CHEST_EXACT[n] if type(cl) == "\115\116\114\105\110\103" then Register(inst, "\099\104\101\115\116\115", cl) else if cl == nil then local low = n:lower() cl = false if low:find("\099\104\101\115\116", 1, true) then local label = "\067\104\101\115\116" if low:find("\108\111\099\107\101\100", 1, true) then label = "\076\111\099\107\101\100\032\067\104\101\115\116" elseif low:find("\118\105\110\101", 1, true) then label = "\086\105\110\101\032\067\104\101\115\116" end cl = { label } end CHEST_EXACT[n] = cl end if cl and not HasTrackedAncestor(inst) then Register(inst, "\099\104\101\115\116\115", cl[1]) end end end end end end local function RegisterDoor(room, door) if not door:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") or door.Name ~= "\068\111\111\114" then return end local num = tonumber(room.Name) local label = "\068\111\111\114\032" .. end end if #panels == 2 then local hm = door:FindFirstChild("\082\052\078\095\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116\077\111\100\101\108") if not hm then hm = Instance.new("\077\111\100\101\108") hm.Name "\072\105\103\104\108\105\103\104\116\080\097\114\116" hp.Transparency = 0.999 hp.Size = p.Size hp.CFrame = p.CFrame hp.CanCollide, hp.CanQuery, hp.CanTouch, hp.CastShadow = false, false, false, false hp.Massless = true hp.Anchored = false hp.Parent = hm local w = Instance.new("\087\101\108\100\067\111\110\115\116\114\097\105\110\116") w.Part0, w.Part1 = hp, p w.Parent = hp end hm.Parent = door end first, RoomNum = num, Door = door }) return end end local leaf = door:FindFirstChild("\068\111\111\114") if leaf and leaf:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then local hp = hp.Transparency = 0.999 hp.Size = leaf.Size hp.CFrame = leaf.CFrame hp.CanCollide, hp.CanQuery, hp.CanTouch, hp.CastShadow = false, false, false, false hp.Massless = true hp.Anchored = false local w = "\100\111\111\114\115", label, { Part = hp, RoomNum = num, Door = door }) return end Register(door, "\100\111\111\114\115", label, { Part = door:FindFirstChild("\068\111\111\114") or GetPart(door), RoomNum = num, Door = door, }) end local function ScanRoom(room) task.spawn(function() local found = room:WaitForChild("\068\111\111\114", 10) for _, c in ipairs(room:GetChildren()) do RegisterDoor(room, c) end if found and found.Parent == room then return end local conn conn = room.ChildAdded:Connect(function(c) RegisterDoor(room, c) if c.Name == "\068\111\111\114" and conn then function Chain(pp) local parts = { pp.Name, pp.ActionText, pp.ObjectText } local cur, d = pp.Parent, 0 while cur and cur ~= Workspace and d < 6 do local n = cur.Name if not (CHAIN_SKIP[n:lower()] or str:gsub("\040\037\108\041\040\037\117\041", "\037\049\032\037\050"):gsub("\091\094\037\097\093\043", "\032"):lower() for w in str:gmatch("\037\097\043") do out[w] = true end return out end local function B.Rooms() return Workspace:FindFirstChild("\067\117\114\114\101\110\116\082\111\111\109\115") end function B.CurRoom() return tonumber(LocalPlayer:GetAttribute("\067\117\114\114\101\110\116\082\111\111\109")) or 0 end function B.FloorName() local gd = RS:FindFirstChild("\071\097\109\101\068\097\116\097") local f = gd and cache, n = {}, 0 end v = s:lower():gsub("\091\094\037\097\093", "") cache[s] = v n = n + 1 end return v end end function B.Has(s, token) return B.Compact(s):find(token, 1, true) ~= nil end function B.InMode(name) return Mode.Name == name or (Mode.All and Mode.All()) or false end function B.RoomOf(inst) local rooms = B.Rooms() local p = inst while p and p.Parent and p.Parent ~= rooms do p = p.Parent end if rooms and p and p.Parent == rooms then return tonumber(p.Name), p end return nil, nil end function B.PosOf(inst) if not inst then return nil end if inst:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") then return inst.Position end if inst:IsA("\065\116\116\097\099\104\109\101\110\116") then return inst.WorldPosition end if inst:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") then local ok, cf = pcall(inst.GetPivot, inst) if ok and cf then return cf.Position end end local part = GetPart(inst) return part and part.Position or nil end function B.Dist(inst) local _, _, root = GetParts() local p = B.PosOf(inst) if root and p then return (p - root.Position).Magnitude end return math.huge end local tpToken, tpHint = 0, false function B.SafeTP(target, hold) local char, _, root = GetParts() if not (char and root) then return false end local goal = typeof(target) == "\067\070\114\097\109\101" and target or CFrame.new(target) local G = GetParts() if r2 and (r2.Position - goal.Position).Magnitude > 3.5 then snap() end end) end) return true end function B.Teleport(pos) local char, _, root = GetParts() if not (char and root) then return false end local dir = root.Position - pos dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z) local off = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit * 3 or Vector3.new(0, 0, 3) local p = pos + off local params = params) local y = hit and (hit.Position.Y + 3.2) or (pos.Y + 3) return B.SafeTP(CFrame.new(p.X, y, p.Z)) end function B.Find(pred, root) local out = {} root = root or B.Rooms() if not root then return out end local n = 0 for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do local ok, hit = pcall(pred, d) if ok and hit then out[#out + 1] = d end n = n + 1 if n % 600 == 0 then task.wait() end end return out end function B.Click(btn) if not btn then return false end local done = false pcall(function() if firesignal then firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) firesignal(btn.Activated) done = true end end) if done = true end end end) end return done end local huds = {} function B.SetHud(id, y, text) if text and text ~= "" then local h = huds[id] if not (h and h.Label.Parent) then local g = FX.JumpLocked then return false end return true end local function StopMove() AD.MoveDir = nil local _, hum, root = GetParts() if hum and root then pcall(function() hum:Move(Vector3.zero, false) hum:MoveTo(root.Position) end) end end local function Fire(pp, times) pcall(function() pp.HoldDuration = 0 pp.RequiresLineOfSight = false end) for _ = 1, times or 3 do if not pp.Parent or not pp.Enabled then break end pcall(fireproximityprompt, pp) task.wait(0.12) end end local function Face(pos, maxWait) AD.Look = pos if Ex.Bot_Camera == false then return end local t0 = os.clock() while os.clock() - t0 < (maxWait or 0.5) do local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera if not cam then return end local dir = pos - cam.CFrame.Position if dir.Magnitude < 0.1 or cam.CFrame.LookVector:Dot(dir.Unit) > 0.96 then return end task.wait(0.05) end end local function Probe(root, dir) RayP.FilterDescendantsInstances = { LocalPlayer.Character } local o = root.Position local d = dir * 3.2 local low = Workspace:Raycast(o - Vector3.new(0, 1.6, 0), d, RayP) local head = Workspace:Raycast(o + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0), d, RayP) return low ~= nil, head ~= nil end local function Arrived(root, dest, o) local near = o.near if near and near.part and near.part.Parent then if (root.Position - near.part.Position).Magnitude <= near.range then return true end end local p = root.Position local flat = Vector3.new(p.X - dest.X, 0, p.Z - dest.Z).Magnitude return flat <= (o.radius or 3) and math.abs(p.Y - dest.Y) <= 8 end local function Follow(wp, dest, o, g, t0, timeout) local lastP, lastT = nil, os.clock() local hits, lastAbort, wt0 = 0, 0, os.clock() while true do if not Alive(g) then return "\115\116\111\112\112\101\100" end local char, hum, root = GetParts() if not root then return "\100\101\097\100" end local now = os.clock() if now - t0 > timeout then return "\116\105\109\101\111\117\116" end if Arrived(root, dest, o) then return "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" end if o.abort and now - lastAbort > 0.2 then lastAbort = now if o.abort() then return "\097\098\111\114\116\101\100" end end local wpos, rp = wp.Position, root.Position local dv = Vector3.new(wpos.X - rp.X, 0, wpos.Z - rp.Z) AD.MoveDir = dv.Magnitude > 0.05 and dv.Unit or nil hum:MoveTo(wpos) AD.Look = Vector3.new(wpos.X, rp.Y + 1.2, wpos.Z) local flat = Vector3.new(rp.X - wpos.X, 0, rp.Z - wpos.Z).Magnitude if flat < math.clamp(hum.WalkSpeed * 0.1, 2.2, 5) then return "\111\107" end if wp.Action == Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump and CanJump() then hum.Jump = true end if now - wt0 > 4.5 then SetCrouch(AD.Sneak) return "\115\116\117\099\107" end lastP = lastP or rp if now - lastT >= 0.5 then local moved = Vector3.new(rp.X - lastP.X, 0, rp.Z - lastP.Z).Magnitude local expect = math.max(hum.WalkSpeed, 8) * 0.5 lastP, lastT = rp, now if moved < expect * 0.15 then hits = hits + 1 local dir = Vector3.new(wpos.X - rp.X, 0, wpos.Z - rp.Z) if dir.Magnitude > 0.1 then local low, head = Probe(root, dir.Unit) if head and not low then SetCrouch(true) elseif low and not head and CanJump() then hum.Jump = true end end if hits >= 3 then SetCrouch(AD.Sneak) return "\115\116\117\099\107" end else hits = 0 Idle() if not AD.Sneak then SetCrouch(false) end end end RunService.Heartbeat:Wait() end end local function Walk(dest, o) o = o or {} local g = AD.Gen local t0 = os.clock() local timeout = o.timeout or 25 local fails, stucks = 0, 0 while true do if not Alive(g) then return "\115\116\111\112\112\101\100" end local _, hum, root = GetParts() if not root then return "\100\101\097\100" end if os.clock() - t0 > timeout then StopMove() return "\116\105\109\101\111\117\116" end if Arrived(root, dest, o) then StopMove() return "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" end if o.abort and o.abort() then StopMove() return "\097\098\111\114\116\101\100" end local path = PathSvc:CreatePath({ AgentRadius = 2, AgentHeight = 5, AgentCanJump = Ex.Bot_Jump ~= false, AgentCanClimb = false, WaypointSpacing = 5, }) local ok = pcall(function() path:ComputeAsync(Ground(root.Position) + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0), dest) end) local wps = (ok and path.Status == Enum.PathStatus.Success) and path:GetWaypoints() or nil if not wps or #wps < 2 then fails = fails + 1 if fails >= 3 then local res = Follow({ Position = dest, Action = Enum.PathWaypointAction.Walk }, dest, o, g, os.clock(), 4) StopMove() if res == "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" or res == "\115\116\111\112\112\101\100" or res == "\100\101\097\100" or res == "\097\098\111\114\116\101\100" then return res end return "\110\111\112\097\116\104" end task.wait(0.25) else local blocked = false local conn = path.Blocked:Connect(function() blocked = true end) local res = "\111\107" for i = 2, #wps do res = Follow(wps[i], dest, o, g, t0, timeout) if res ~= "\111\107" or blocked then break end end conn:Disconnect() if res == "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" or res == "\115\116\111\112\112\101\100" or res == "\100\101\097\100" or res == "\116\105\109\101\111\117\116" or res == "\097\098\111\114\116\101\100" then StopMove() return res end if res == "\115\116\117\099\107" then stucks = stucks + 1 if stucks >= 4 then StopMove() return "\115\116\117\099\107" end end end end end local function Threat() local _, _, root = GetParts() if not root then return nil end local best local now = os.clock() for _, c in ipairs(Workspace:GetChildren()) do if DANGER[c.Name] and c:IsA("\077\111\100\101\108") and c:GetAttribute("\073\110\097\099\116\105\118\101") ~= true then local pp = c.PrimaryPart or c:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") if pp then local d = (pp.Position - root.Position).Magnitude local rec = AD.Ent[c] if not rec then rec = { d = d, t = now, spd = 0 } AD.Ent[c] = rec elseif now - rec.t > 0.15 then local closing = (rec.d - d) / (now - rec.t) rec.spd = rec.spd * 0.6 + math.max(closing, 0) * 0.4 rec.d, rec.t = d, now end local sp = math.max(rec.spd, (BASE_SPEED[c.Name] or 80) * 0.6) local eta = d / sp if not best or eta < best.eta then best = { model = c, name = c.Name, dist = d, eta = eta } end end end end return best end local function ScanRoom(room) local now = os.clock() local c = AD.Spots[room] if c and now - c.t < 6 then return c.list end local list = {} for _, d in ipairs(room:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") and (d.Name == "\072\105\100\101\080\114\111\109\112\116" or d.Name == "\072\105\100\105\110\103\080\114\111\109\112\116") then local model = d:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("\077\111\100\101\108") local part = d.Parent if not (part and part:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116")) then part = model and (model.PrimaryPart or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116", true)) end if model and part then list[#list + 1] = { prompt = d, model = model, part = part } end end end AD.Spots[room] = { t = now, list = list } return list end local function BestSpot(pos) local rooms = RoomsFolder() if not rooms then return nil end local cur = CurRoom() local best, bestD = nil, math.huge local now = os.clock() for _, room in ipairs(rooms:GetChildren()) do local n = tonumber(room.Name) if n and (not cur or math.abs(n - cur) <= 1) then for _, s in ipairs(ScanRoom(room)) do local p = s.prompt if p.Parent and s.part.Parent and p.Enabled and (AD.BadSpot[s.model] or 0) < now then local sp = s.part.Position if sp.Y > -10 and math.abs(sp.Y - pos.Y) < 9 then local hp = s.model:FindFirstChild("\072\105\100\100\101\110\080\108\097\121\101\114", true) if not (hp and hp.Value) then local d = (sp - pos).Magnitude if d < bestD then best, bestD = s, d end end end end end end end return best, bestD end local function HideLead(root, hum) local spot, d = BestSpot(root.Position) if not spot then return nil, nil end return d * 1.4 / math.max(hum.WalkSpeed, 10) + (Ex.Bot_HideMargin or 4), spot end local function DangerAbort() if not Ex.AD_Avoid then return false end local th = Threat() if not th then return false end local _, hum, root = GetParts() if not root then return false end local lead = HideLead(root, hum) if not lead then return false end return th.eta <= lead + 0.5 end local function HideFrom(th) local g = AD.Gen local attempts = 0 while Alive(g) and attempts < 3 do attempts = attempts + 1 local char, hum, root = GetParts() if not root then return false end local spot = BestSpot(root.Position) if not spot then return false end AD.Info = "\071\111\105\110\103\032\116\111\032\104\105\100\101\032\102\114\111\109\032" .. th.name local rng = math.clamp((spot.prompt.MaxActivationDistance or 8) - 1.2, 3, 8) Walk(Ground(spot.part.Position), { radius = 2.5, timeout = 14, near = { part = spot.part, range = rng } }) if not Alive(g) then return false end char = LocalPlayer.Character if not char then return false end Face(spot.part.Position, 0.35) for _ = 1, 8 do if char:GetAttribute("\072\105\100\105\110\103") then break end Fire(spot.prompt, 1) task.wait(0.15) end if char:GetAttribute("\072\105\100\105\110\103") then AD.Stats.Hides = AD.Stats.Hides + 1 local t0, calmFrom, lastName = os.clock(), nil, th.name while Alive(g) do if not (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:GetAttribute("\072\105\100\105\110\103")) then return true end local t = Threat() if t then calmFrom, lastName = nil, t.name AD.Info = string.format("\072\105\100\100\101\110\058\032\037\115\032\105\115\032\099\111\109\105\110\103\032\040\126\037\046\048\102\115\041", t.name, t.eta) else calmFrom = calmFrom or os.clock() AD.Info = "\072\105\100\100\101\110\058\032\119\097\105\116\105\110\103\032\102\111\114\032\105\116\032\116\111\032\098\101\032\103\111\110\101" if os.clock() - calmFrom >= (REBOUND[lastName] and 4 or 2.2) then break end end if os.clock() - t0 > (Ex.AD_MaxHide or 40) then break end Idle() task.wait(0.15) end for _ = 1, 6 do if not (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:GetAttribute("\072\105\100\105\110\103")) then break end ExitCloset() task.wait(0.5) end Mark() return true end AD.BadSpot[spot.model] = os.clock() + 30 end return false end local function IsLadderPrompt(pp) local cur = pp.Parent for _ = 1, 5 do if not cur then break end local n = cur.Name:lower() if n:find("\108\097\100\100\101\114", 1, true) or n:find("\099\108\105\109\098", 1, true) then return true end cur = cur.Parent end return pp.Name:lower():find("\099\108\105\109\098", 1, true) ~= nil end local function TryLadder() if Ex.AD_Ladders == false then return false end local rooms, cur = RoomsFolder(), CurRoom() local _, _, root = GetParts() if not (rooms and cur and root) then return false end local best, bd for _, room in ipairs(rooms:GetChildren()) do local n = tonumber(room.Name) if n and math.abs(n - cur) <= 1 then for _, d in ipairs(room:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") and d.Enabled and d.Parent and d.Parent:IsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116") and IsLadderPrompt(d) then local dist = (d.Parent.Position - root.Position).Magnitude if not bd or dist < bd then best, bd = d, dist end end end end end if not best then return false end local th = Ex.AD_Avoid and Threat() or nil if th and th.eta < 25 then return false end local part = best.Parent AD.Info = "\085\115\105\110\103\032\097\032\108\097\100\100\101\114" local r = Walk(Ground(part.Position), { radius = 3, timeout = 15, abort = DangerAbort, near = { part = part, range = math.max(3, (best.MaxActivationDistance or 8) - 1) }, }) if r ~= "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" then return false end Face(part.Position, 0.4) Fire(best, 2) local t0 = os.clock() task.wait(1) local char = LocalPlayer.Character while char and os.clock() - t0 < 12 and char:GetAttribute("\067\108\105\109\098\105\110\103") do Idle() task.wait(0.2) end Mark() return true end local function PromptOf(e) local p = e.Prompt if p and p.Parent and p:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") then return p end local inst = e.Inst if inst and inst.Parent then for _, d in ipairs(inst:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") and d.Enabled and d.Name ~= "\072\105\100\101\080\114\111\109\112\116" then return d end end end return nil end local function NextTarget(cur, rp, mode) local best, bestPrompt, bestScore = nil, nil, math.huge local maxTry = Ex.AD_Retries or 3 local prio = Ex.AD_LootOrder == PRIO_LABEL for inst, e in pairs(Tracked) do local opt = KIND[e.Cat] local on = opt and (Ex[opt] or ((mode == "\107\110\111\098" or mode == "\115\101\097\114\099\104") and KNOB[e.Cat])) if on and inst.Parent and (AD.Tries[inst] or 0) < maxTry and (mode ~= "\107\101\121\115" or e.Cat == "\107\101\121\115") and (mode == "\107\101\121\115" or RoomOf(inst) == cur) then local skip = e.Cat == "\105\116\101\109\115" and Ex.AIItemIgnore and Ex.AIItemIgnore[e.Label] if (e.Cat == "\099\104\101\115\116\115" or e.Cat == "\108\111\099\107\101\114\115") and inst:GetAttribute("\076\111\099\107\101\100") == true then skip = true end if not skip then local pp = PromptOf(e) if pp and pp.Enabled and e.Part and e.Part.Parent then local score = (e.Part.Position - rp).Magnitude if prio then score = score + (PRIO[e.Cat] or 2) * 100000 end if score < bestScore then best, bestPrompt, bestScore = e, pp, score end end end end end return best, bestPrompt end local function Visit(e, pp) local inst, part = e.Inst, e.Part AD.Tries[inst] = (AD.Tries[inst] or 0) + 1 if not (part and part.Parent) then return false end local rng = math.clamp((pp.MaxActivationDistance or 8) - 1.2, 3, 9) local res = Walk(Ground(part.Position), { radius = 2.5, timeout = 22, abort = DangerAbort, near = { part = part, range = rng } }) if res == "\110\111\112\097\116\104" then TryLadder() return false end if res ~= "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" then return false end Face(part.Position, 0.5) task.wait(0.05 + (Ex.AD_Delay or 350) / 4000) Fire(pp, 3) if not pp.Parent or not pp.Enabled or not inst.Parent then AD.Tries[inst] = 99 AD.Stats.Loot = AD.Stats.Loot + 1 Mark() return true end return false end local function IsOpen(door, lv0) return (Latest() or 0) > lv0 or door:GetAttribute("\079\112\101\110\101\100") == true or door:GetAttribute("\079\112\101\110") == true end local function WaitOpen(door, lv0, secs) local t0 = os.clock() while os.clock() - t0 < secs and Ex.AutoDoors do if IsOpen(door, lv0) then return true end task.wait(0.15) end return IsOpen(door, lv0) end local function DoorFront(door, rootPos) local cf = door:GetPivot() local a, b = (cf * CFrame.new(0, 0, 6)).Position, (cf * CFrame.new(0, 0, -6)).Position local p = ((a - rootPos).Magnitude <= (b - rootPos).Magnitude) and a or b return Ground(p) end local function DoorStep(startRoom) local _, _, root = GetParts() if not root then return false end local door = NextClosedDoor() if not door then AD.Info = "\087\097\105\116\105\110\103\032\102\111\114\032\116\104\101\032\110\101\120\116\032\114\111\111\109\032\116\111\032\108\111\097\100" task.wait(0.4) return false end local lv0 = Latest() or 0 local locked = door:FindFirstChild("\076\111\099\107") ~= nil if locked then if not Ex.AD_Keys then AD.Info = "\076\111\099\107\101\100\032\100\111\111\114\058\032\116\117\114\110\032\111\110\032\071\114\097\098\032\075\101\121\115\032\038\032\085\110\108\111\099\107\032\068\111\111\114\115" Idle() return false end if not (HasItem("\075\101\121") or HasItem("\075\101\121\066\097\099\107\100\111\111\114")) then AD.WantKey = true AD.KeyWaitSince = AD.KeyWaitSince or os.clock() if os.clock() - AD.KeyReset > 15 then AD.KeyReset = os.clock() AD.Tries = NewTries() end local waited = os.clock() - AD.KeyWaitSince AD.Info = string.format("\076\111\099\107\101\100\032\100\111\111\114\058\032\115\101\097\114\099\104\105\110\103\032\102\111\114\032\097\032\107\101\121\032\040\037\100\115\041", math.floor(waited)) if Ex.AD_KeyWait ~= false or waited < 30 then if waited < 180 then Idle() end end return false end AD.WantKey, AD.KeyWaitSince = false, nil end local dpart = door.PrimaryPart or door:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("\066\097\115\101\080\097\114\116", true) AD.Info = "\087\097\108\107\105\110\103\032\116\111\032\116\104\101\032\100\111\111\114" local res = Walk(DoorFront(door, root.Position), { radius = 3, timeout = 45, near = dpart and { part = dpart, range = 8 } or nil, abort = function() return DangerAbort() or CurRoom() ~= startRoom end, }) if res == "\110\111\112\097\116\104" then TryLadder() end if res ~= "\097\114\114\105\118\101\100" then return false end if dpart then Face(dpart.Position, 0.6) end if locked then for _, d in ipairs(door:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") and d.Name == "\085\110\108\111\099\107\080\114\111\109\112\116" then Fire(d, 2) end end task.wait(0.3) end local n = 0 for _, d in ipairs(door:GetDescendants()) do if d:IsA("\080\114\111\120\105\109\105\116\121\080\114\111\109\112\116") and d.Enabled and d.Name ~= "\085\110\108\111\099\107\080\114\111\109\112\116" and d.Name ~= "\072\105\100\101\080\114\111\109\112\116" then Fire(d, 2) n = n + 1 if n >= 4 then break end end end local opened = WaitOpen(door, lv0, 4) if not opened and Ex.AD_ForceOpen then local co = door:FindFirstChild("\067\108\105\101\110\116\079\112\101\110") if co and co:IsA("\082\101\109\111\116\101\069\118\101\110\116") then AD.Info = "\068\111\111\114\032\115\116\117\099\107\058\032\102\111\114\099\105\110\103\032\105\116\032\111\112\101\110" pcall(function() co:FireServer() end) opened = WaitOpen(door, lv0, 2.5) end end if opened then AD.DoorFails = 0 AD.Stats.Doors = AD.Stats.Doors + 1 Mark() else AD.DoorFails = AD.DoorFails + 1 AD.Info = "\068\111\111\114\032\100\105\100\032\110\111\116\032\111\112\101\110\032\040\116\114\121\032" .. AD.DoorFails .. "\041" end return opened end local function TP(cf) local B8 = FX.B8 if B8 and B8.SafeTP then return B8.SafeTP(cf, 0.6) end local char = LocalPlayer.Character if char then char:PivotTo(cf) return true end return false end AD.Recover = function(why) AD.Stuck = AD.Stuck + 1 AD.Stats.Fixes = AD.Stats.Fixes + 1 local lvl = AD.Stuck AD.Tries = NewTries() AD.Room, AD.WantKey, AD.KeyWaitSince = nil, false, nil AD.DoorFails = 0 AD.BadSpot = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" }) AD.Spots = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "\107" }) StopMove() if lvl >= 2 then AD.NoLootUntil = os.clock() + 40 ExitCloset() SetCrouch(false) end if lvl >= 3 then Release() task.wait(0.4) pcall(Sync) local door = NextClosedDoor() local co = door and door:FindFirstChild("\067\108\105\101\110\116\079\112\101\110") if Ex.AD_ForceOpen and co and co:IsA("\082\101\109\111\116\101\069\118\101\110\116") then pcall(function() co:FireServer() end) end end if lvl >= 4 then local _, hum = GetParts() if hum then local rv = Vector3.new(math.random(-10, 10) / 10, 0, math.random(-10, 10) / 10) if rv.Magnitude < 0.2 then rv = Vector3.new(1, 0, 0) end AD.MoveDir = rv.Unit task.wait(0.5) AD.MoveDir = nil end end if lvl >= 5 and Ex.AD_TPFallback then local rooms = RoomsFolder() local latest = Latest() local room = rooms and latest and rooms:FindFirstChild(tostring(latest)) local anchor = room and (room:FindFirstChild("\082\111\111\109\069\110\116\114\097\110\099\101") or room:FindFirstChild("\068\111\111\114")) if anchor then TP(anchor:GetPivot() * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0)) end end AD.Progress = os.clock() if lvl == 8 then Say("\083\116\105\108\108\032\115\116\117\099\107\032\097\102\116\101\114\032\056\032\097\117\116\111\045\102\105\120\101\115\046\032\067\104\101\099\107\032\116\104\101\032\114\111\111\109\032\109\097\110\117\097\108\108\121\032\040\112\117\122\122\108\101\032\047\032\108\111\099\107\101\100\032\100\111\111\114\063\041\046", true) else Say("\065\117\116\111\045\102\105\120\032\035" .. lvl .. "\032\040" .. tostring(why) .. "\041") end end local function UpdateFigure(cur) if os.clock() - AD.FigureT < 1 then return end AD.FigureT = os.clock() local rooms = RoomsFolder() local room = rooms and cur and rooms:FindFirstChild(tostring(cur)) AD.FigureNear = room ~= nil and (room:FindFirstChild("\070\105\103\117\114\101\082\105\103", true) ~= nil or room:FindFirstChild("\070\105\103\117\114\101\082\097\103\100\111\108\108", true) ~= nil) end local function KnobFarmStep(cur, root) if AD.KnobDone then AD.Info = "\075\110\111\098\032\070\097\114\109\032\114\117\110\110\105\110\103" Idle() return true end if (Latest() or 0) ~= 0 or cur ~= 0 then SetFeature("\065\068\095\075\110\111\098\070\097\114\109", false) Say("\075\110\111\098\032\070\097\114\109\032\077\111\100\101\032\110\101\101\100\115\032\121\111\117\032\105\110\032\082\111\111\109\032\048\046\032\084\117\114\110\101\100\032\111\102\102\046", true) return true end local e, pp if os.clock() - AD.RoomT < math.max(Ex.AD_RoomTime or 30, 20) then e, pp = NextTarget(cur, root.Position, "\107\110\111\098") end if e then AD.Info = "\075\110\111\098\032\070\097\114\109\058\032\099\111\108\108\101\099\116\105\110\103\032" .. tostring(e.Label) Visit(e, pp) task.wait((Ex.AD_Delay or 350) / 1000) return true end local gold = Gold() if gold and gold > 0 and FX.StartKnobFarm then AD.KnobDone = true SetFeature("\075\110\111\098\070\097\114\109", true) FX.StartKnobFarm() Say("\075\110\111\098\032\070\097\114\109\032\115\116\097\114\116\101\100\032\119\105\116\104\032" .. tostring(gold) .. "\032\103\111\108\100\046", true) else SetFeature("\065\068\095\075\110\111\098\070\097\114\109", false) Say("\075\110\111\098\032\070\097\114\109\058\032\110\111\032\103\111\108\100\032\102\111\117\110\100\032\105\110\032\082\111\111\109\032\048\044\032\110\111\116\104\105\110\103\032\116\111\032\102\097\114\109\046", true) end return true end local function Step() if IsLobby() then AD.Info = "\073\110\032\116\104\101\032\108\111\098\098\121" Idle() return end local char, hum, root = GetParts() if not (char and hum and root) then AD.Info = "\087\097\105\116\105\110\103\032\102\111\114\032\121\111\117\114\032\099\104\097\114\097\099\116\101\114" Idle() return end if hum.Health <= 0 then AD.WasDead = true if Ex.AD_StopOnDeath then SetFeature("\065\117\116\111\068\111\111\114\115", false) Say("\089\111\117\032\100\105\101\100\058\032\065\117\116\111\032\068\111\111\114\115\032\116\117\114\110\101\100\032\111\102\102\046", true) return end AD.Info = "\068\101\097\100\058\032\119\097\105\116\105\110\103\032\116\111\032\114\101\115\112\097\119\110" Idle() return end if AD.WasDead then AD.WasDead = false AD.Room, AD.Tries, AD.WantKey = nil, NewTries(), false AD.Crouching = false Say("\066\097\099\107\032\097\108\105\118\101\058\032\065\117\116\111\032\068\111\111\114\115\032\114\101\115\117\109\101\100\046") Mark() end if (Ex.AD_LowHP or 0) > 0 and hum.MaxHealth > 0 and (hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth) * 100 < Ex.AD_LowHP then SetFeature("\065\117\116\111\068\111\111\114\115", false) Say("\072\101\097\108\116\104\032\098\101\108\111\119\032" .. Ex.AD_LowHP .. "\037\058\032\065\117\116\111\032\068\111\111\114\115\032\116\117\114\110\101\100\032\111\102\102\046", true) return end if (Ex.AD_StopMinutes or 0) > 0 and os.clock() - AD.Stats.Start >= Ex.AD_StopMinutes * 60 then SetFeature("\065\117\116\111\068\111\111\114\115", false) Say("\084\105\109\101\032\108\105\109\105\116\032\114\101\097\099\104\101\100\058\032\065\117\116\111\032\068\111\111\114\115\032\116\117\114\110\101\100\032\111\102\102\046", true) return end if Sync() then AD.Info = "\082\111\111\109\115\058\032\065\117\116\111\032\082\111\111\109\115\032\105\115\032\100\114\105\118\105\110\103" Idle() return end local cur, latest = CurRoom(), Latest() if not (cur and latest) then AD.Info = "\087\097\105\116\105\110\103\032\102\111\114\032\103\097\109\101\032\100\097\116\097" return end UpdateFigure(cur) AD.Sneak = AD.FigureNear and Ex.Bot_Crouch ~= false SetCrouch(AD.Sneak) ApplySpeed() local th = Ex.AD_Avoid and Threat() or nil if th then local lead = HideLead(root, hum) if lead then if AD.SpoofVoted then AD.SpoofVoted = false if FX.SpoofVote then FX.SpoofVote("\098\111\116", false) end end if th.eta <= lead then HideFrom(th) return end AD.Info = string.format("\037\115\032\105\110\032\126\037\046\048\102\115\058\032\119\111\114\107\105\110\103\032\110\101\097\114\032\097\032\099\108\111\115\101\116", th.name, th.eta) else AD.Info = th.name .. "\032\105\115\032\099\111\109\105\110\103\032\097\110\100\032\116\104\101\114\101\032\105\115\032\110\111\032\099\108\111\115\101\116\058\032\117\115\105\110\103\032\080\111\115\105\116\105\111\110\032\083\112\111\111\102" if not AD.SpoofVoted then AD.SpoofVoted = true if FX.SpoofVote then FX.SpoofVote("\098\111\116", true) end end end elseif AD.SpoofVoted then AD.SpoofVoted = false if FX.SpoofVote then FX.SpoofVote("\098\111\116", false) end end if char:GetAttribute("\072\105\100\105\110\103") then if os.clock() - AD.LastExit > 1 then AD.LastExit = os.clock() ExitCloset() end AD.Info = "\076\101\097\118\105\110\103\032\116\104\101\032\099\108\111\115\101\116" return end if latest ~= AD.LastLatest then AD.LastLatest = latest Mark() end if Ex.AD_StopRoom > 0 and latest >= Ex.AD_StopRoom then SetFeature("\065\117\116\111\068\111\111\114\115", false) Say("\082\101\097\099\104\101\100\032\114\111\111\109\032" .. latest .. "\058\032\065\117\116\111\032\068\111\111\114\115\032\116\117\114\110\101\100\032\111\102\102\046", true) return end local now = os.clock() if cur ~= AD.Room then AD.Room, AD.RoomT, AD.WantKey, AD.DoorFails = cur, now, false, 0 end if Ex.AD_KnobFarm then KnobFarmStep(cur, root) return end if now >= AD.NoLootUntil then local e, pp if now - AD.RoomT < Ex.AD_RoomTime then e, pp = NextTarget(cur, root.Position, nil) end if not e and AD.WantKey then e, pp = NextTarget(cur, root.Position, "\107\101\121\115") if not e then e, pp = NextTarget(cur, root.Position, "\115\101\097\114\099\104") end end if e then AD.Info = "\076\111\111\116\105\110\103\032" .. tostring(e.Label) Visit(e, pp) task.wait(Ex.AD_Delay / 1000) return end end if Ex.AD_Doors then DoorStep(cur) task.wait(Ex.AD_Delay / 1000) else AD.Info = "\073\100\108\101\032\040\079\112\101\110\032\068\111\111\114\115\032\105\115\032\111\102\102\041" Idle() end end local function Cleanup() AD.Active = false AD.MoveDir = nil AD.SpeedNoted = false FX.BotSpeed = nil FX.NormalWS = nil SetCrouch(false) AD.Look, AD.CamRot = nil, nil if AD.SpoofVoted then AD.SpoofVoted = false if FX.SpoofVote then FX.SpoofVote("\098\111\116", false) end end local _, hum = GetParts() if hum then StopMove() if not Ex.SpeedHack and AD.OrigWS then pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = AD.OrigWS end) end end Release() end local function StartLoop() AD.Gen = AD.Gen + 1 local my = AD.Gen AD.Beat = os.clock() task.spawn(function() while Alive(my) do AD.Beat = os.clock() if AD.RecoverWhy then local why = AD.RecoverWhy AD.RecoverWhy = nil pcall(AD.Recover, why) end local ok, err = pcall(Step) if ok then AD.Errors = 0 task.wait(0.2) else AD.Errors = AD.Errors + 1 AD.Info = "\069\114\114\111\114\058\032" .. tostring(err) if AD.Errors % 5 == 0 then AD.RecoverWhy = "\101\114\114\111\114\115" end task.wait(math.min(0.5 + AD.Errors * 0.25, 3)) end end end) task.spawn(function() while Alive(my) do task.wait(0.5) if not Alive(my) then return end pcall(AD.UpdateHUD) if os.clock() - AD.Beat > 60 then AD.Stats.Fixes = AD.Stats.Fixes + 1 AD.Progress = os.clock() Say("\084\104\101\032\108\111\111\112\032\102\114\111\122\101\058\032\114\101\115\116\097\114\116\105\110\103\032\065\117\116\111\032\068\111\111\114\115\046", true) StartLoop() return end if Ex.AD_Watchdog and not AD.RecoverWhy and os.clock() - AD.Progress > (Ex.AD_StuckTime or 15) then AD.RecoverWhy = "\110\111\032\112\114\111\103\114\101\115\115\032\102\111\114\032" .. tostring(Ex.AD_StuckTime) .. "\115" end end end) end Hooks.AD_HUD = function() pcall(AD.UpdateHUD) end Hooks.AutoDoors = function(v) AD.Gen = AD.Gen + 1 if v then local any = false for _, k in ipairs(OPTS) do if Ex[k] then any = true break end end if not any then AD.Recommended(false) end AD.Tries = NewTries() AD.Room, AD.Info = nil, "\083\116\097\114\116\105\110\103" AD.Stuck, AD.Errors, AD.RecoverWhy = 0, 0, nil AD.WantKey, AD.KeyWaitSince, AD.KeyReset, AD.NoLootUntil = false, nil, 0, 0 AD.DoorFails, AD.WasDead, AD.KnobDone, AD.Crouching = 0, false, false, false AD.LastLatest = Latest() AD.Progress = os.clock() AD.Stats = { Doors = 0, Loot = 0, Fixes = 0, Hides = 0, Start = os.clock() } local _, hum = GetParts() AD.OrigWS = hum and (Saved.HackWS or hum.WalkSpeed) or 16 FX.NormalWS = AD.OrigWS AD.Running, AD.Active = true, true Say("\077\111\100\101\058\032" .. ModeNm() .. "\046\032\084\104\101\032\098\111\116\032\105\115\032\114\117\110\110\105\110\103\046") StartLoop() else Cleanup() AD.Info = "\079\102\102" if AD.Running then AD.Running = false Say("\083\116\111\112\112\101\100\046\032" .. StatsLine()) end pcall(AD.UpdateHUD) end end end)() local B64R = {} for i = 1, 64 do B64R[B64:sub(i, i)] = i - 1 end local function b64enc(s) local out = {} for i = 1, #s, 3 do local a, b, c = s:byte(i, i + 2) local v = a * 65536 + (b or 0) * 256 + (c or 0) local c1 = math.floor(v / 262144) % 64 local c2 = math.floor(v / 4096) % 64 local c3 = math.floor(v / 64) % 64 local c4 = v % 64 out[#out + 1] = B64:sub(c1 + 1, c1 + 1) .. B64:sub(c2 + 1, c2 + 1) .. (b and B64:sub(c3 + 1, c3 + 1) or "") .. (c and B64:sub(c4 + 1, c4 + 1) or "") end return table.concat(out) end local function b64dec(s) s = s:gsub("\091\094\037\119\037\045\095\093", "") local out = {} for i = 1, #s, 4 do local c1, c2 = B64R[s:sub(i, i)], B64R[s:sub(i + 1, i + 1)] local c3, c4 = B64R[s:sub(i + 2, i + 2)], B64R[s:sub(i + 3, i + 3)] if not (c1 and c2) then return nil end local v = c1 * 262144 + c2 * 4096 + (c3 or 0) * 64 + (c4 or 0) out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(v / 65536) % 256) if c3 then out[#out + 1] = string.char(math.floor(v / 256) % 256) end if c4 then out[#out + 1] = string.char(v % 256) end end return table.concat(out) end local function lzwEncode(s) local dict, size = {}, 256 for i = 0, 255 do dict[string.char(i)] = i end local w, out = "", {} for i = 1, #s do local c = s:sub(i, i) local wc = w .. c if dict[wc] then w = wc else out[#out + 1] = dict[w] if size < 65535 then dict[wc] = size size = size + 1 end w = c end end if w ~= "" then out[#out + 1] = dict[w] end return out end local function lzwDecode(codes) local dict, size = {}, 256 for i = 0, 255 do dict[i] = string.char(i) end local w = dict[codes[1]] if not w then return nil end local out = { w } for i = 2, #codes do local k = codes[i] local entry if dict[k] then entry = dict[k] elseif k == size then entry = w .. w:sub(1, 1) else return nil end out[#out + 1] = entry if size < 65535 then dict[size] = w .. entry:sub(1, 1) size = size + 1 end w = entry end return table.concat(out) end local function PackCode(json) local codes = lzwEncode(json) local bytes = {} for i, c in ipairs(codes) do bytes[i] = string.char(math.floor(c / 256), c % 256) end return "\082\052\078\050\045" .. b64enc(table.concat(bytes)) end local function UnpackCode(code) local body = tostring(code or ""):gsub("\037\115\043", i = 1, #raw, 2 do local a, b = raw:byte(i, i + 1) codes[#codes + 1] = a * 256 + b end return lzwDecode(codes) end local function HttpReq(opts) local f = (syn and syn.request) or request or
